wet_brt_start_crisis = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      tag = A15
      has_war_with = A20
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_crisis_started
      }
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_brt_crisis_started
    set_global_flag = wet_story_bretonnian_civil_war
    set_variable = {
      wet_brt_disorder_days = 180
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_contingency_council
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_contingency_council
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_artois_loyal
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_artois_loyal
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_lyonesse_loyal
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_lyonesse_loyal
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_carcassonne_loyal
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_carcassonne_loyal
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_loyal_captains
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_loyal_captains
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_armouries_audited
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_armouries_audited
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_regional_war_plans
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_regional_war_plans
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_royal_couriers
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_royal_couriers
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_shrines_guarded
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_shrines_guarded
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_fay_protected
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_fay_protected
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_necromancers_mapped
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_necromancers_mapped
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_fully_prepared
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_fully_prepared
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_fully_prepared
      }
      set_variable = {
        wet_brt_disorder_days = 0
      }
    }
    else = {
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_contingency_council
        }
        add_stability = -0.15
      }
      else = {
        add_stability = -0.2
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_royal_couriers
        }
        add_war_support = -0.05
      }
      else = {
        add_war_support = -0.15
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          has_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_artois_loyal
        }
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          check_variable = { num_owned_states
          >
          1 }
          34 = {
            is_owned_by = A15
            is_fully_controlled_by = A15
            is_capital = no
            has_border_war = no
            NOT = {
              any_state_division = {
                always = yes
              }
            }
          }
        }
        A20 = {
          transfer_state = 34
        }
        set_country_flag = wet_brt_defected_45
      }
      else = {
        set_country_flag = wet_brt_defection_blocked_45
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          has_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_lyonesse_loyal
        }
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          check_variable = { num_owned_states
          >
          1 }
          529 = {
            is_owned_by = A15

wet_brt_refresh_disorder = {
  set_variable = {
    wet_brt_disorder_attack = -0.15
  }
  set_variable = {
    wet_brt_disorder_org = -0.2
  }
  set_variable = {
    wet_brt_disorder_output = -0.2
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_regional_war_plans
    }
    set_variable = {
      wet_brt_disorder_attack = -0.05
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_loyal_captains
    }
    set_variable = {
      wet_brt_disorder_org = -0.1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_brt_snapshot_armouries_audited
    }
    set_variable = {
      wet_brt_disorder_output = -0.1
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      wet_brt_civil_war = yes
      check_variable = {
        wet_brt_disorder_days
        >
        0
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          has_dynamic_modifier = wet_brt_disorder
        }
      }
      add_dynamic_modifier = {
        modifier = wet_brt_disorder
      }
    }
  }
  else = {
    remove_dynamic_modifier = {
      modifier = wet_brt_disorder
    }
  }
}

              }

wet_brt_resolve_duel = {
  wet_brt_refresh_rulers = yes
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_brt_duel_begun
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_duel_resolved
      }
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_brt_duel_resolved
    if = {
      limit = {
        wet_brt_duel_valid = yes
      }
      random_list = {
        50 = {
          set_country_flag = wet_brt_green_dead
          set_country_flag = wet_brt_duel_green_lost
          retire_character = WET_BRT_GreenKnight
          set_variable = {
            wet_brt_broken_days = 180
          }
          add_ideas = wet_brt_broken
          add_war_support = -0.15
          country_event = {
            id = wet_bretonnia.101
          }
        }
        50 = {
          set_country_flag = wet_brt_duel_green_won
          A20 = {
            if = {
              limit = {
                has_government = democratic
              }
              party_leader = {
                limit = {
                  has_ideology = democratic
                  has_character_flag = wet_brt_duel_incumbent
                }
                set_character_flag = wet_brt_dead_in_duel
              }
            }
            if = {
              limit = {
                has_government = fascism
              }
              party_leader = {
                limit = {
                  has_ideology = fascism
                  has_character_flag = wet_brt_duel_incumbent
                }
                set_character_flag = wet_brt_dead_in_duel
              }
            }
            if = {
              limit = {
                has_government = communism
              }
              party_leader = {
                limit = {
                  has_ideology = communism
                  has_character_flag = wet_brt_duel_incumbent
                }
                set_character_flag = wet_brt_dead_in_duel
              }
            }
            if = {
              limit = {
                has_government = neutrality
              }
              party_leader = {
                limit = {
                  has_ideology = neutrality
                  has_character_flag = wet_brt_duel_incumbent
                }
                set_character_flag = wet_brt_dead_in_duel
              }
            }
            if = {
              limit = {
                has_government = waaagh
              }
              party_leader = {
                limit = {
                  has_ideology = waaagh
                  has_character_flag = wet_brt_duel_incumbent
                }
                set_character_flag = wet_brt_dead_in_duel
              }
            }
            if = {
              limit = {
                has_government = horned_rat
              }
              party_leader = {
                limit = {
                  has_ideology = horned_rat
                  has_character_flag = wet_brt_duel_incumbent
                }
                set_character_flag = wet_brt_dead_in_duel
              }
            }
            set_country_flag = wet_brt_needs_regency
            kill_country_leader = yes
            set_variable = {
              wet_brt_broken_days = 180
            }
            add_ideas = wet_brt_broken
            add_war_support = -0.15
            wet_brt_regency_a20 = yes
          }
          country_event = {
            id = wet_bretonnia.102
          }
        }
      }
    }
    else = {
      set_country_flag = wet_brt_duel_cancelled
    }
  }
}
wet_brt_tick_broken = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      check_variable = {
        wet_brt_broken_days
        >
        0
      }
      has_war_with = A15
    }
    subtract_from_variable = {
      wet_brt_broken_days = 1
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          has_idea = wet_brt_broken
        }
      }
      add_ideas = wet_brt_broken
    }
  }
  else = {
    set_variable = {
      wet_brt_broken_days = 0
    }
    remove_ideas = wet_brt_broken
  }
}
wet_brt_send_settlement = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      tag = A20
      exists = yes
      NOT = {
        has_war_with = A15
      }
      A15 = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_crisis_started
        capital_scope = {
          is_fully_controlled_by = A15
        }
      }
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_offer_settlement
      }
      check_variable = {
        wet_brt_offer_settlement_cooldown
        <
        1
      }
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_offer_settlement_accepted
      }
      OR = {
        NOT = {
          has_country_flag = wet_brt_offer_settlement_attempted
        }
        A15 = {
          has_political_power
          >
          -1
        }
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_offer_settlement_attempted
      }
      A15 = {
        add_political_power = -0
      }
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_brt_offer_settlement_attempted
    set_country_flag = wet_brt_offer_settlement
    set_variable = {
      wet_brt_offer_settlement_days = 30
    }
    A15 = {
      if = {
        limit = {
          NOT = {
            is_in_array = {
              wet_brt_active_recipients = PREV
            }
          }
        }
        add_to_array = {
          wet_brt_active_recipients = PREV
        }
      }
    }
    country_event = {
      id = wet_bretonnia.103
    }
  }
}
wet_brt_barrow_fall = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      tag = A74
      FROM = {
        tag = A15
      }
      capital_scope = {
        is_fully_controlled_by = A15
      }
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_court_intercepted
      }
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_brt_court_intercepted
    capital_scope = {
      set_state_flag = wet_brt_barrow_court
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_character = WET_A74_leader
      }
      capital_scope = {
        set_state_flag = wet_brt_kemmler_prison
      }
      WET_A74_leader = {
        set_nationality = A15
      }
      A15 = {
        set_country_flag = wet_brt_kemmler_custody
        add_to_variable = {
          wet_brt_kemmler_generation = 1
        }
      }
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_character = WET_BRT_Krell
      }
      capital_scope = {
        set_state_flag = wet_brt_krell_prison
      }
      WET_BRT_Krell = {
        set_nationality = A15
      }
      A15 = {
        set_country_flag = wet_brt_krell_custody
        add_to_variable = {
          wet_brt_krell_generation = 1
        }
      }
    }
    A15 = {
      remove_country_leader_role = {
        character = WET_A74_leader
        ideology = wet_unaligned_vampiric
      }
      set_country_flag = wet_brt_barrow_location
    }
  }
}
wet_brt_refresh_prisons = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_brt_barrow_location
    }

              has_country_flag = wet_brt_kemmler_custody
            }

              has_country_flag = wet_brt_kemmler_custody
            }
          }
          every_country = {

              has_country_flag = wet_brt_kemmler_custody

          }
          set_country_flag = wet_brt_kemmler_custody
          A15 = {
            add_to_variable = {
              wet_brt_kemmler_generation = 1
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    every_state = {
      limit = {
        has_state_flag = wet_brt_krell_prison
      }
      controller = {
        if = {
          limit = {
            NOT = {
              has_country_flag = wet_brt_krell_custody
            }
            any_country = {
              has_country_flag = wet_brt_krell_custody
            }
          }
          every_country = {
            limit = {
              has_country_flag = wet_brt_krell_custody
            }
            clr_country_flag = wet_brt_krell_custody
            if = {
              limit = {
                has_character = WET_BRT_Krell
              }
              WET_BRT_Krell = {
                set_nationality = PREV.PREV
              }
            }
          }
          set_country_flag = wet_brt_krell_custody

              wet_brt_krell_generation = 1
            }
          }
        }

    }
  }
}

wet_brt_relocate_staff = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_brt_staff_custodian
      has_country_flag = wet_brt_staff_move_authorised
      capital_scope = {
        is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
      }
      check_variable = {
        wet_brt_staff_operation_generation = global.wet_brt_staff_generation
      }
    }
    every_state = {
      limit = {
        has_state_flag = wet_brt_staff_site
      }
      clr_state_flag = wet_brt_staff_site
    }
    capital_scope = {
      set_state_flag = wet_brt_staff_site
    }
    add_to_variable = {
      global.wet_brt_staff_generation = 1
    }
    clr_country_flag = wet_brt_staff_move_authorised
  }
}
wet_brt_take_staff = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_brt_staff_operation_succeeded
      check_variable = {
        wet_brt_staff_operation_generation = global.wet_brt_staff_generation
      }
      OR = {
        any_state = {
          has_state_flag = wet_brt_staff_site
          is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
        }
        AND = {
          tag = U24
          has_country_flag = wet_ngs_guaranteed_staff_recovery
        }
      }
    }
    every_country = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_staff_custodian
      }
      clr_country_flag = wet_brt_staff_custodian
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_brt_staff_custodian
    add_to_variable = {
      global.wet_brt_staff_generation = 1
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          tag = A15
        }
        316 = {
          NOT = {
            is_fully_controlled_by = A15
          }
          has_state_flag = wet_brt_staff_site
        }
      }
      set_global_flag = wet_story_la_maisontaal_lost
    }
  }
  clr_country_flag = wet_brt_staff_operation_succeeded
}
wet_brt_measure_bloc = {
  set_variable = {
    wet_brt_defending_manpower = 0
  }
  for_each_scope_loop = {
    array = wet_brt_war_target
    every_country = {
      limit = {
        exists = yes
        NOT = {
          tag = A15
        }
        OR = {
          tag = PREV
          is_subject_of = PREV
          is_in_faction_with = PREV
          PREV = {
            is_guaranteed_by = PREV
          }
        }
      }
      A15 = {
        add_to_variable = {
          var = wet_brt_defending_manpower
          value = PREV.deployed_army_manpower_k
        }
      }
    }
  }
  multiply_variable = {
    wet_brt_defending_manpower = 1.25
  }
}

wet_brt_refresh_outward = {
  for_each_scope_loop = {
    array = wet_brt_bonus_enemies
    A15 = {
      remove_dynamic_modifier = {
        modifier = wet_brt_errentry_target
        scope = PREV
      }
      remove_dynamic_modifier = {
        modifier = wet_brt_vanguard_target
        scope = PREV
      }
    }
  }
  clear_array = wet_brt_bonus_enemies
  every_enemy_country = {
    limit = {
      wet_brt_everchosen_belligerent = yes
    }
    A15 = {
      add_to_array = {
        wet_brt_bonus_enemies = PREV
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          check_variable = {
            wet_brt_days_errentry
            >
            0
          }
        }
        add_dynamic_modifier = {
          modifier = wet_brt_errentry_target
          scope = PREV
        }
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_brt_vanguard_bonus
          wet_brt_joint_war = yes
        }
        add_dynamic_modifier = {
          modifier = wet_brt_vanguard_target
          scope = PREV
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

wet_focus_actions_bretonnia = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_brt_guard_staff_focus_running
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          AND = {
            has_global_flag = wet_brt_staff_exists
            any_state = {
              has_state_flag = wet_brt_staff_site
              is_owned_by = A15
              is_fully_controlled_by = A15
            }
            check_variable = {
              wet_brt_staff_operation_generation = global.wet_brt_staff_generation
            }
          }
        }
      }
      clr_country_flag = wet_brt_guard_staff_focus_running
      set_variable = {
        wet_brt_guard_staff_focus_days = 0
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_brt_guard_staff_active
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_brt_guard_staff_active
      }
    }
    else = {
      subtract_from_variable = {
        wet_brt_guard_staff_focus_days = 1
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          check_variable = {
            wet_brt_guard_staff_focus_days
            <
            1
          }
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_brt_guard_staff_focus_running
        set_variable = {
          wet_brt_guard_staff_focus_days = 0
        }
        if = {
          limit = {
            has_country_flag = wet_brt_guard_staff_active
          }
          clr_country_flag = wet_brt_guard_staff_active
          if = {
            limit = {
              has_global_flag = wet_brt_staff_exists
              any_state = {
                has_state_flag = wet_brt_staff_site
                is_owned_by = A15
                is_fully_controlled_by = A15
              }
              check_variable = {
                wet_brt_staff_operation_generation = global.wet_brt_staff_generation
              }
            }
            set_country_flag = wet_brt_staff_operation_succeeded
            wet_brt_take_staff = yes
            if = {
              limit = {
                wet_brt_staff_safe = yes
              }
              set_country_flag = wet_brt_staff_defended
            }
            set_country_flag = wet_brt_guard_staff_done
          }
          else = {
            
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  else = {
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_guard_staff_focus_pending
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          always = no
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_brt_guard_staff_focus_pending
      }
      else_if = {
        limit = {
          has_completed_focus = WET_BRT_K06
          NOT = {
            has_country_flag = wet_brt_guard_staff_done
          }
          any_state = {
            has_state_flag = wet_brt_staff_site
            is_owned_by = A15
            is_fully_controlled_by = A15
          }
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_brt_guard_staff_focus_pending
        set_country_flag = wet_brt_guard_staff_focus_running
        set_variable = {
          wet_brt_guard_staff_focus_days = 30
        }
        set_country_flag = wet_brt_guard_staff_active
        set_variable = {
          wet_brt_staff_operation_generation = global.wet_brt_staff_generation
        }
      }
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_brt_search_barrow_court_focus_running
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          AND = {
            wet_brt_court_access = yes
          }
        }
      }
      clr_country_flag = wet_brt_search_barrow_court_focus_running
      set_variable = {
        wet_brt_search_barrow_court_focus_days = 0
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_brt_search_barrow_court_active
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_brt_search_barrow_court_active
      }
    }
    else = {
      subtract_from_variable = {
        wet_brt_search_barrow_court_focus_days = 1
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          check_variable = {
            wet_brt_search_barrow_court_focus_days
            <
            1
          }
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_brt_search_barrow_court_focus_running
        set_variable = {
          wet_brt_search_barrow_court_focus_days = 0
        }
        if = {
          limit = {
            has_country_flag = wet_brt_search_barrow_court_active
          }
          clr_country_flag = wet_brt_search_barrow_court_active
          if = {
            limit = {
              wet_brt_court_access = yes
            }
            set_country_flag = wet_brt_court_searched
            set_country_flag = wet_brt_search_barrow_court_done
          }
          else = {
            
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  else = {
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_search_barrow_court_focus_pending
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          always = no
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_brt_search_barrow_court_focus_pending
      }
      else_if = {
        limit = {
          has_completed_focus = WET_BRT_K00
          NOT = {
            has_country_flag = wet_brt_search_barrow_court_done
          }
          wet_brt_court_access = yes
          NOT = {
            has_country_flag = wet_brt_court_searched
          }
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_brt_search_barrow_court_focus_pending
        set_country_flag = wet_brt_search_barrow_court_focus_running
        set_variable = {
          wet_brt_search_barrow_court_focus_days = 7
        }
        set_country_flag = wet_brt_search_barrow_court_active
      }
    }
  }
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_focus_running
    }
    if = {
      limit = {
        NOT = {
          AND = {
            wet_brt_kemmler_captive = yes
            check_variable = {
              wet_brt_hunt_generation = wet_brt_kemmler_generation
            }
          }
        }
      }
      clr_country_flag = wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_focus_running
      set_variable = {
        wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_focus_days = 0
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          has_country_flag = wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_active
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_active
        set_country_flag = wet_brt_kemmler_escaped
      }
    }
    else = {
      subtract_from_variable = {
        wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_focus_days = 1
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          check_variable = {
            wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_focus_days
            <
            1
          }
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_focus_running
        set_variable = {
          wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_focus_days = 0
        }
        if = {
          limit = {
            has_country_flag = wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_active
          }
          clr_country_flag = wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_active
          if = {
            limit = {
              wet_brt_kemmler_captive = yes
              check_variable = {
                wet_brt_hunt_generation = wet_brt_kemmler_generation
              }
            }
            set_global_flag = wet_brt_kemmler_dead
            every_country = {
              limit = {
                has_country_flag = wet_brt_kemmler_custody
                has_character = WET_A74_leader
              }
              retire_character = WET_A74_leader
              clr_country_flag = wet_brt_kemmler_custody
            }
          }
          else = {
            set_country_flag = wet_brt_kemmler_escaped
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  else = {
    if = {
      limit = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_focus_pending
      }
      if = {
        limit = {
          always = no
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_focus_pending
      }
      else_if = {
        limit = {
          has_completed_focus = WET_BRT_K10
          wet_brt_kemmler_captive = yes
          check_variable = {
            wet_brt_hunt_generation
            <
            wet_brt_kemmler_generation
          }
          has_political_power
          >
          74
        }
        clr_country_flag = wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_focus_pending
        set_country_flag = wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_focus_running
        set_variable = {
          wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_focus_days = 30
        }
        add_political_power = -75
        set_country_flag = wet_brt_hunt_kemmler_active
        set_variable = {
          wet_brt_hunt_generation = wet_brt_kemmler_generation
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

            }
            AND = {
              is_in_faction_with = A01
              A01 = {
                exists = yes
                has_government = democratic
                has_completed_focus = WET_REI_E24
                has_completed_focus = WET_REI_E14
              }
              A15 = {
                NOT = {
                  OR = {
                    threat
                    <
                    0.25
                  }
                }
                wet_rei_war_veto_clear = yes
              }
            }
          }
          NOT = {
            has_country_flag = wet_brt_offer_alliance
          }
          check_variable = {
            wet_brt_offer_alliance_cooldown
            <
            1
          }
          A15 = {
            NOT = {
              has_country_flag = wet_brt_pending_alliance
            }
          }
          OR = {
            NOT = {
              has_country_flag = wet_brt_offer_alliance_attempted
            }
            A15 = {
              has_political_power
              >
              -1
            }
          }
        }
      }
      random_country = {
        limit = {
          wet_brt_grand_leader = yes
          NOT = {
            has_war_with = A15
          }
          NOT = {
            any_enemy_country = {
              tag = A15
            }
          }
          A15 = {
            is_subject = no
            is_in_faction = no
            has_government = democratic
            NOT = {
              any_enemy_country = {
                is_in_faction_with = PREV.PREV
              }
            }
          }
          OR = {
            NOT = {
              OR = {
                threat
                <
                1
              }
            }
            AND = {
              is_in_faction_with = A01
              A01 = {
                exists = yes
                has_government = democratic
                has_completed_focus = WET_REI_E24
                has_completed_focus = WET_REI_E14
              }
              A15 = {
                NOT = {
                  OR = {
                    threat
                    <
                    0.25
                  }
                }
                wet_rei_war_veto_clear = yes
              }
            }
          }
          NOT = {
            has_country_flag = wet_brt_offer_alliance
          }
          check_variable = {
            wet_brt_offer_alliance_cooldown
            <
            1
          }
          A15 = {
            NOT = {
              has_country_flag = wet_brt_pending_alliance
            }
          }
          OR = {
            NOT = {
              has_country_flag = wet_brt_offer_alliance_attempted
            }
            A15 = {
              has_political_power
              >
              -1
            }
          }
        }
        wet_brt_send_alliance = yes
      }
    }
    every_other_country = {
      limit = {
        exists = yes
        is_in_faction_with = A15
        NOT = {
          tag = A15
        }
        NOT = {
          has_war_with = A15
        }
        NOT = {
          has_country_flag = wet_karaz_sealed
        }
      }
      save_event_target_as = wet_focus_recipient
      A15 = {
        if = {
          limit = {
            has_completed_focus = WET_BRT_O05
            event_target:wet_focus_recipient = {
              exists = yes
              is_in_faction_with = A15
              NOT = {
                tag = A15
              }
              NOT = {
                has_war_with = A15
              }
              NOT = {
                has_country_flag = wet_karaz_sealed
              }
              NOT = {
                has_country_flag = wet_brt_offer_relief_host
              }
              check_variable = {
                wet_brt_offer_relief_host_cooldown
                <
                1
              }
              OR = {
                NOT = {
                  has_country_flag = wet_brt_offer_relief_host_attempted
                }
                A15 = {
                  has_political_power
                  >
                  -1
                }
              }
            }
          }
          event_target:wet_focus_recipient = {
            wet_brt_send_relief_host = yes
          }
        }
      }
    }
    wet_brt_reconcile_benefits = yes
  }
}

  }

    if = {

  }

wet_brt_award_O09 = {
  if = {
    limit = {
      has_country_flag = wet_brt_earned_O09
      NOT = {
        has_country_flag = wet_brt_reward_O09
      }
      wet_brt_expedition_ready = yes
    }
    set_country_flag = wet_brt_reward_O09
    set_country_flag = wet_brt_expedition_open
    random_other_country = {
      limit = {
        wet_brt_everchosen = yes
      }
      save_event_target_as = wet_focus_recipient
      A15 = {
        if = {
          limit = {
            has_completed_focus = WET_BRT_O09
            event_target:wet_focus_recipient = {
              wet_brt_everchosen = yes
              NOT = {
                has_war_with = A15
              }
              NOT = {
                is_in_faction_with = A15
              }
              NOT = {
                has_truce_with = A15
              }
              NOT = {
                has_non_aggression_pact_with = A15
              }
              A15 = {
                can_declare_war_on = PREV
              }
            }
            wet_brt_expedition_ready = yes
            has_political_power
            >
            24
          }
          add_political_power = -25
          create_wargoal = {
            type = annex_everything
            target = event_target:wet_focus_recipient
            expire = 180
          }
        }
      }
    }
    wet_brt_reconcile_benefits = yes
  }
}
